2 Kings 6–7 · grammar, 5–30%

9 from-scratch questions × 7 conditions × 7 reader settings = 441 responses. Conditions are clean and six independently selected grammar doses. All defect versions passed the current grammar and meaning checks.

Dose counts affected clean Chinese source characters, with ±2 percentage-point tolerance. The generation bank uses a soft preference of 30% misselection and 70% misordering by error instances. Accuracy is against the clean-source key. E (“无法判断”), wrong A–D answers, and invalid responses are reported separately. One answer changes a score by 11.1 percentage points; this small run is exploratory.

Same seven model settings and reader-specific prompts as prior dose experiments; temperature 0, 8192-token context, 2500 generated-token cap including thinking. Fresh conversation per question and condition; raw responses and thinking retained. Clean baseline included. Exact request checkpoints reused only when resuming this run. Unmatched nonempty answers from the first four settings use the established GPT-4.1-mini choice mapper, receiving only response text and choices. No passages were published.

当时，城门入口处有四个患麻风病的人。他们彼此说：“我们为什么坐在这里等死呢？ 如果我们说：‘进城去吧’，城里有饥荒，我们必死在那里；如果坐在这里，我们也必死。来吧，我们到亚兰人的营地去。他们若让我们活，我们就活；他们若杀我们，我们就死。”

那几个患麻风病的人到了营地边缘，进入一顶帐篷，又吃又喝。随后，他们拿走银子、金子和衣服，去藏了起来。回来后，他们又进了另一顶帐篷，从那里拿走一些东西，也藏了起来。 最后，他们彼此说：“我们这样做不对。今天是个有好消息的日子。如果我们不作声，等到天亮，我们的罪必追上我们。现在，我们去向王宫报信吧。”

麻风病的人承认自己做错了之后打算做什么？

  1. 去向王宫报信。
  2. 等到天亮再去报信。
  3. 把藏起来的财物送回去。
  4. 回到帐篷里寻找食物。
  5. 无法判断
